Optional 10x features: visual regression, accessibility, security, performance, regression analysis, CI generation, auto-heal, HTML dashboard, and contract testing. - **informed-explore**: Like `explore`, but INFORMED: every agent first reads a briefing of recurring CPython C bug SHAPES (sibling-hunt templates with their guarded twins), the cross-cutting triage rules, and the false-positive taxonomy — so it confirms-without-relitigating, suppresses known FPs, and hunts un-found siblings of known shapes instead of re-discovering from scratch. Use for a thorough audit, a re-review of a subsystem, or whenever fix-propagation matters more than raw speed. ([ReviewToolkits/cpython-review-toolkit](https://github.com/ReviewToolkits/cpython-review-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/cpython-review-toolkit/commands/informed-explore.md) · ★10)
